                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 12.21                           | Airport coordinates are **collected, not bundled**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `payload[1]` carries the code, full name, city, country and coordinates of every airport in the query, and the adapter had been discarding all of it. Before this the repository knew `LIM` only as three letters matching a regular expression. The alternative was a bundled IATA table of 40–60 kB — heavier than the entire map — which would also have needed maintaining. They go to one `airports.json` for the whole archive rather than one per route, because an airport is a fact about the world and LIM appearing in nine watched pairs should not be nine copies that can disagree. This is also the one part of the archive that is genuinely re-fetchable, so it is written whole rather than appended.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 12.22                           | The map is **d3-geo on a canvas with the arcs in SVG**, not a tile engine.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | The mature component in this space is MapLibre-based and measured at 275 kB gzip — 2.8x the whole app bundle — and it reaches three third-party hosts by default (CARTO tiles, unpkg for its worker, jsdelivr for world geometry), each of which this repository forbids. Escaping all three means passing a blank style, self-hosting the worker and bundling our own geometry, at which point the tile renderer has no tiles and what remains is its arc layer. Measured alternative: 13 kB of code and 39 kB of geometry, no network ever, and every arc is a DOM node a test can query — the same argument 12.12 made for the price chart. `d3-scale` was already a dependency, so `d3-geo` is a sibling rather than a new class of thing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 12.23                           | Arcs are **great circles**, and nothing on the map animates by itself.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | A curve drawn in longitude and latitude is indistinguishable from the real track on LIM-CUZ and visibly wrong on LIM-MAD; the reference component still ships the wrong one, with the fix sitting in an unmerged pull request. Static dashes rather than a flowing animation is not only taste: with nothing animating, the render loop runs _only_ while a pointer is down, so an idle map costs zero CPU and a drag gets the whole frame budget. During a drag the SVG is hidden and the arcs go to the canvas — thirty attribute writes a frame is invisible at five routes and not at forty.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 12.24                           | 1:110m geography, not 1:50m.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Measured: `countries-110m` is 39 kB gzip, `countries-50m` is 236 kB — as heavy as the whole tile engine this design was chosen to avoid. Detailed geography is expensive whatever draws it, and at the size a route map is read, 110m coastlines are not the limiting factor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 12.25                           | The analysis is a **band with a median**, with the provider's own history dashed behind it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | A single cheapest-per-period line cannot tell a real fall from a board whose expensive itineraries sold out — the two move for different reasons and look identical plotted as one number. The band is the range of the cheapest fare within the period and the line is its median, chosen over the mean so one collection during a fare glitch cannot drag a week. Candles were considered and rejected: open and close only mean something if you observed at both ends of the period, which write-on-change does not promise, so the shape would claim a precision the data does not have. The provider's baseline stays a separate dashed series for the reason 12.19 already gave — at day granularity it is one rounded integer with no band possible.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 12.26                           | Only the open route's arc is coloured by its variation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Colouring every arc needs every route's latest price and typical, which is one history request per watched route — and this page holds one archive at a time by design. A neutral arc therefore means _not looked up_, not _around usual_, and the legend says so. A route-summary endpoint would fix it; inventing a colour from data we did not fetch would not.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 12.27                           | Place names **fade from the geometry**, not from a stylesheet.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `facesViewer` answers yes right up to 90° and no immediately after, which is correct for an arc and wrong for a name: it blinks out mid-rotation. Opacity is recomputed every frame from angular distance to the limb, so at 60 fps the fade is a continuous function of where the globe is pointing. A CSS transition cannot do this on its own — the node unmounts, and a transition on a mounting node never runs — so it is kept only for the jumps geometry does not cover, a zoom press or a change of projection. Measured over one drag: South America 0.55 → 0.44 → 0.22 → 0.03 → gone, with Oceania coming up the other way.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 12.28                           | Country names are **derived from the outlines already drawn**, and gated on zoom as well as on room.                                                                                                                                                                                                           | mapcn reads place names from a basemap's symbol layers; a blank style has none, for mapcn as much as for us. But `countries-110m` already carries a name per shape, so 176 labels cost zero additional bytes — positioned at the largest polygon's centroid, because a whole-shape centroid puts France four degrees out in the Atlantic. A name appears once the ground under it is big enough to hold it, estimated from the projection's local area scale factor (`r²·cos θ` orthographic, `s²·sec²φ` Mercator) rather than by streaming a polygon: checked against `geoPath.area` on six real countries in both projections, within 5%, at one cosine per label. Room alone was not enough — Russia and Brazil have room in the very first frame — so the layer is also gated on zoom, and crosses over with the continents at about 2.2×.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 12.29                           | A blocked name **steps aside**; the airport codes never move.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | The codes are the data the map exists for, so they claim their ground first. Culling the loser was the first attempt and was wrong in the one case that matters: LIM sits almost exactly on the middle of South America, so the continent this reader is looking at became the one continent with no name on it. Standard cartographic practice is that the label moves, and one or two line heights is enough.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| 12.30                           | The flat map's zoom **multiplies the fit** instead of being capped by it, and the pan clamp covers both axes.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | The scale read `min(width / 2π, radius · 0.6)` with the zoom already inside `radius` — and `width / 2π` is exactly the scale that fits 360° of longitude across the frame, so the cap bit at about 1.1× and the flat map stopped zooming there while the globe went on to 8×. With zoom applied to the unzoomed fit instead, the map becomes wider than the frame, which makes sideways worth reaching. Rather than a hard axis lock, the clamp measures the projected sphere on both axes: at the default zoom that still reads as up and down only, because there genuinely is nothing to reach sideways.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 12.31                           | The overlay commits **synchronously inside the frame callback**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `draw` paints the canvas inside the callback, but a plain `setState` only schedules a React render, and React's scheduler runs on a task the browser reaches after it has already painted — so the land moved on one frame and the arcs, the airport codes and the place names on the next. One frame of slip at 60 Hz is small and entirely visible: the map slides out from under its own labels for as long as the drag lasts. `flushSync` costs the same work undeferred, and only ever runs while a pointer is down. The CSS opacity transition is disabled during a drag for the same reason — with geometry setting opacity every frame, a transition on top is a low-pass filter and the names fade behind the globe they belong to.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 12.32                           | Arc colour is **route identity**, built rather than picked — superseding the cheap/dear tone of 12.26.                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Every route on this page leaves from the same origin, so the arcs go out as a fan; in one accent they are one shape. 12.26 coloured the open route by how far its fare sat from usual and left the rest neutral, which meant seven identical lines and a signal that only ever applied to one of them. Colour now says _which_ route and nothing about price — the detail panel says that in words and a number, where there is room. Seven hues evenly around the wheel from the app's own accent, each fitted to the same perceived luminance as that accent (matched to within 0.002) at one moderate saturation, so none outshines the others and only temperature separates them. Emitted as a stride across the wheel rather than around it, which lifts the smallest gap between neighbouring routes from 41 to 113 in RGB. A first hand-picked set failed its own check — amber and copper sat 22 apart. The watchlist carries the same colour beside each row, so the mapping is visible rather than deduced.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 12.33                           | A watched route is **one line**: colour, pair, departure, return, remove.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Three stacked lines per route made the panel beside the map scroll at four entries. On one line the legs are marked by direction — up for the way out, down for the way back — with the arrows hidden from the accessibility tree and the words carried beside them, because "up arrow 2026-10-17" is not what a departure date sounds like. Measured after the first pass: with both legs shown the row had one pixel to spare, which is a coincidence rather than a layout, so the dates and gaps came down again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 12.34                           | A route is picked by **pressing its line**, and only on the side of the globe facing you.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Settled on the way up rather than with a click handler on the path: the stage captures the pointer the moment a drag starts, and a captured pointer sends its click to the capturing element instead of the path it began on — so the arc's own handler is unreliable exactly while the map is in use. Comparing where a press started and ended also draws the line that matters: still is a choice, travelled is a drag. The far half of an arc is drawn — the globe is glass — but offers no target, because picking a line you are looking at the back of would mean clicking through whatever is genuinely in front of it. Measured live: 11 arc runs on the globe, 3 of them behind, 8 targets, none on a behind run; a press selects, a drag across the same line does not.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 12.35                           | The map is not text: `user-select: none` on the stage.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Everything on it is a handle, not a passage. Without it a drag across a continent name sweeps a selection highlight instead of turning the globe, and a double-click leaves half the labels highlighted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 12.36                           | Dates read `dd/mm/yyyy`, converted by splitting the string.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `YYYY-MM-DD` is what goes to disk and what sorts and compares correctly, so it stays that everywhere but on screen. Not via `Date`: parsing `2026-10-17` gives midnight UTC, and rendering that anywhere west of Greenwich — Lima, for one — shows the 16th. Anything that is not a plain calendar date comes back untouched, because a date the reader can see and puzzle over beats a silent blank.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 12.37                           | A suggestion list is **told** which way to open, not left to measure.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | The old version read the list's own rectangle after it had painted and moved it on the next frame, so the first open always drew off the right edge first — and drawing off the right edge is what puts a horizontal scrollbar on the page. Where a field sits in its row is something the caller knows for free: origin opens rightwards from its left edge, destination is pinned to its right edge and grows leftwards. Measured live: the destination list's right edge is flush with its field's (1460), its left edge 143px further left, and the document's scroll width equals its client width with either list open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 12.38                           | The map and the watchlist are **two cells of one grid row**, so they share a height.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Previously two stacked columns, each growing to its own content, which left the shorter one with a ragged gap beside the other. As grid children with the row stretching, whichever has more to say sets the height and the other fills it; the watchlist scrolls inside it while the fields above stay put, because a list long enough to scroll is exactly when you least want the way to add to it scrolled away.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 12.39                           | "Add route" lives in the panel header, tied to its form by `form`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | The action belongs at the top of the panel it acts on rather than buried under the fields, and a submit button carries a `form` attribute for exactly this — the association is stated instead of inherited from the tree. The unit tests render that button beside the editor rather than working around its absence, because the association is the part of this arrangement that can silently stop working. The page subtitle went at the same time: it described what the page obviously does, and the room was worth more than the sentence.
